## How to Review

1. Open each draft file in `C:\Users\Michael\Documents\device_catalog\pipeline\data\drafts`
2. Search for `[NEEDS CONTENT` to find placeholder sections
3. Fill in clinical detail from IFU PDFs or other sources
4. When satisfied, move the file to the catalog root directory
5. Rename the paired PDF (if downloaded) to match the naming convention
